No.'->REFUSAL, 'cant sleep'->BLOCK all correct); (2) the wall is ENTIRELY NO_FIRE (18/22, no cue present); (3) reading the 18 NO_FIRE items, they decompose into: **DEEP INFERENCE = the dominant core** (outcome states an EVENT from which the goal-relevant RESULT must be inferred, NO cue possible: 'walked away'->didn't-knock-out, 'now I gotta do it by myself'->failed-to-avoid, 'that is when it started'->didn't-cool-down) + polysemy/achieve-cue-gaps ('got it'/'managed' -- ambiguous, M3-inc1 already proved pool-expansion FLAT) + idioms ('kabash') + non-aligned/noisy Evidence. **=> the 'extraction' wall is NOT clause-selection, NOT classification (0 wrong), NOT cue-coverage (flat) -- it is DEEP SITUATION-MODEL INFERENCE (outcome-event -> goal-relevant-result), the genuine irreducible-comprehension core (matches the adversarial brain-drill's 'the 45% residual is documented-hard-for-humans-too' finding). This STRENGTHENS the B recommendation: the residual is not a tractable front-end fix -- it is genuine comprehension-inference, neither cheap nor clearly tractable; bank the validated architecture + auditability edge.**

**✅ GOAL-RELATIVE ACTION-RELATION beats valence on the real residual (2026-08-08 self-drive, USER rec B, Director-VET'd, HARNESS-BUG-CAUGHT; report-only scratchpad/goal_relative_recurrence_v1.py): the differentiated edge is REAL on real data.** REAL-DATA-FIRST (read the 15 valence-blind DesireDB residual items) REDIRECTED B: the residual is NOT world-knowledge scripts (race/job/exam) -- it is the GOAL<->OUTCOME ACTION RELATION: did the wanted action RECUR ("met up", "did just that", "took what was offered" = MET) or was it NEGATED/REFUSED/BLOCKED ("I couldn't", "would not give", "held his course", "but no...had to go" = UNMET). Built a goal-relative recurrence+negation detector (find_desired_state goal verb + WordNet verb-synonyms + pro-form "did that" + owned _verb_negated_before/refusal cues). **HARNESS-BUG CAUGHT (the arc's core lesson AGAIN): first run scored 0.200 because it compared the organ's 'MET'/'UNMET' to the benchmark's 'Fulfilled'/'Unfulfilled' without MAPPING -- an under-VET'd false-negative I caught by READING per-item output (pred='UNMET' vs gold='Unfulfilled').** CORRECTED: **accuracy WHEN IT FIRES = 5/6 = 0.833** on the residual where VALENCE is at chance (rule 0.429); overall 8/15=0.533 (abstains -> majority). => the goal<->outcome ACTION relation does what valence CANNOT, on real data. Coverage-gated: 9/15 non-fire = 3 goal-recognition MISSES ('ached to'/'asked for'/negated-goal -> FIXABLE goal-reader coverage) + 6 deep-pragmatic abstains (substitution/contrast/inference = the hard tail); 1 wrong fire (spurious synonym-recurrence 'say hello'). HONEST: n=6 fires is TINY (directional, not conclusive) -- but it is REAL data + the brain-foundational-right mechanism (ACC monitors whether the intended ACT occurred, not its valence) + can-fail. **STRATEGIC (stabilizing): the substrate now has TWO goal-achievement channels -- VALENCE (the ~83% bulk, ~rule-level) + GOAL-RELATIVE ACTION-RELATION (the residual, differentiated, 0.83-when-fires); COMBINED could match/slightly-beat the rule on DesireDB, and the relation channel is LARGER on narrative (intended-action recurrence/negation dominates plot).** NEXT: (1) extend goal-recognition coverage ('ache to'/'ask for'/negated-goal); (2) integrate valence + relation into ONE system, re-score full 80 vs rule.

Grounding BOTTOMS OUT at the tiny seed -- you can't compose meaning from a definition when the definition's words aren't grounded either. **=> THE LEARNING MECHANISM IS SOUND + PRECISE, but definition-composition needs a SUBSTANTIAL GROUNDED CORE to compose FROM; a 23-word seed can't reach anything. The 'self-extending stand-alone tool' = the learning mechanism (proven) + a large SUPPLIED grounded core (Warriner 13,915 lemmas V/A/D + Binder/CSLB feature norms) = the GROUNDING FLOOR, not hand-fed vocab. Answers the USER architecture question: learning IS right + works, but grounding must bottom out in a supplied core. NEXT (the real vocab fix): supply the large grounded core (Warriner/Binder as DATA -> FPE axes at scale) THEN the definition-composition learner self-extends beyond it. **➡️ A-STEP-1b LEXICON-OVER-TAGGER GATE OVERRIDE (2026-08-08 self-drive, Director-VET'd, scratchpad/lexicon_over_tagger_gate.py) = HONEST PARTIAL, precision CLEAN, bottleneck walks DOWN a 3rd level.** At the governing-verb gate, accept a token as a governing verb if it's in the closed DESIDERATIVE_PASS/REQUEST lexicon EVEN IF the POS-tagger disagrees -- BUT only in a genuine VERB SYNTACTIC POSITION (precision guard: REJECT if a determiner/possessive dependent precedes v; ACCEPT only if v structurally governs an nsubj-left or obj-right). ROOT CAUSE of the 3 misses (ONE mechanism, diagnosed): arc-parser produces a NON-TREE 2-CYCLE on 'DET-subject + adjacent mistagged-NOUN-verb' ('The prisoners yearn for open sky' -> heads cycle prisoners<->yearn) which CORRUPTS the determiner attachment (det attaches to the verb-token) -> the det_left guard then rejects on corrupted parse. **Deeper fix = arc-parser MST/tree-consistency DECODING (eliminate 2-cycles), NOT the gate.** **=> BOTTLENECK HAS WALKED DOWN 3 LEVELS: (1) missing construction [FIXED], (2) tagger mistags governing verb [lexicon-override, mostly FIXED], (3) arc-parser non-tree decoding degeneracy [the next residual]. This 3-level descent CONFIRMS the lit-drill's central claim: FRONT-END (POS+parse) FIDELITY is the structural glass-box wall -- the field uses embeddings/LLMs precisely because they win on this extraction step.** DIRECTOR DECISION (USER 'your rec' -> A'): STOP the front-end whack-a-mole (grinding a from-scratch parser's decoder = diminishing returns + where glass-box structurally loses); the goal-recognition fixes are net-positive + wire-ready (held in scratchpad); PIVOT to A' = the PUBLIC BENCHMARK (DesireDB, BOTH arms: oracle-extraction isolates the collapse-mechanism/seam + full-pipeline gives the honest front-end-limited number) vs baselines, inspectable-trace as differentiator. ## 🧪 ACQUISITION INCREMENT 1 = HARD_FAIL (informative, NOT a ceiling) + BRAIN-FOUNDATIONAL DRILL + RE-SPEC (2026-08-06, commit 7c314c840, Director disk-VET'd incl cert 220/3 ran myself + Channel-B earned-theta confirmed)
USER greenlit "build it, no corners cut, brain-foundational." Built per pre-reg (both channels, reused organs verbatim, Channel B valence from FROZEN reward-earned theta -- confirmed: RECIPROCITY -0.9951 vs BLOCK_HIGH +0.6119, random-theta ~0; NO hand-assigned polarity; 3 corner-temptations resisted+reported). **VERDICT HARD_FAIL (measured, honest): heldout 2/7 (POS 2/4=earn,gain; NEG 0/3), sole HARD_FAIL cause anti-drift leak noise=2/8; scramble collapses 0.143; ablation A_only=4/7 (chance/majority-class artifact -- Channel A carries ZERO valence signal, 129POS/120NEG across 249 episodes) vs B_only=2/7 vs combined=2/7.** Cert 220/3 (Director ran on clean tree, both edits present). Commit clean (6 files, no clobber). **BRAIN-FOUNDATIONAL DRILL (SHAPE+POSITION+METRIC -- this is the payoff, NOT a real falsification of reward-grounding): (1) SHAPE wrong -- we tried to acquire a verb's POLARITY as a LEXICAL property, but for a reward-grounded system (and the brain) outcome-valence is RELATIONAL (value of outcome vs GOAL): "earned a prize"(POS) vs "earned a scolding"(NEG) -- polarity is NOT in "earn". (2) POSITION wrong -- we tested Channel B (a goal-CONGRUENCE mechanism) on BARE GOAL-LESS clauses (goal_sentences empty) -> it was denied its required input -> degenerated to conflating transitivity/agonist-realization with goal-completion (leaks POS onto neutral transitives answer/carry, misses intransitive NEG desert/waste/fade). (3) METRIC wrong -- scored context-free local verb polarity vs a rubric, ill-defined for goal-relative verbs. So the 'falsification' is correctly SCOPED to 'bare corpus clauses' -- reward-PE-grounds-GOAL-RELATIVE-outcome-valence was NEVER fairly tested (no goal context). BRAIN EXISTENCE-PROOF HOLDS: not a ceiling, a mis-specified/unfair test on thin dated data.** USER STEER (2026-08-06, integrated): make it FAIR + McGuffey is dated + do we have enough data + the brain can do it so we can. **DATA INVENTORY (measured on disk): raw text ABUNDANT + far better/more-MODERN corpora already present than McGuffey (onestop=modern graded, simplewiki, race=modern comprehension, litbank_coref_conll=modern annotated narrative, + goal-DENSE novels anne_of_green_gables/little_women/tom_sawyer/alice/wizard_of_oz). BINDING CONSTRAINT is NOT raw text -- it's GOAL-STRUCTURED ANNOTATED EVAL passages: we have only 10 real (real_text_goal_owner_diagnostic_v1) + 62 fair-instrument. So NOT data-starved on exposure; UNDER-resourced on a goal-bearing EVAL set.** **RE-SPEC (increment 1b, brain-foundational + fair + modern): (a) acquire the verb's TELICITY/RESULT-STATE features (LEXICAL/structural -- what syntactic-bootstrapping ACTUALLY predicts, Channel A redirected from valence->telicity), NOT polarity; (b) compute goal-relative MET/UNMET via the ALREADY-WIRED congruence_outcome_valence organ IN a goal context; (c) test in GOAL-BEARING PASSAGES from a MODERN goal-dense corpus (drop McGuffey), built into a larger FAIR eval. Reuses even more owned machinery; fixes SHAPE+POSITION+METRIC at once.** Registry row grounded_word_acquisition_loop_increment1 = gate SHELVE (revival = telicity discriminator + goal-passage structure), gitignored/local.
**✅ FAIR MODERN GOAL-BEARING EVAL BUILT + Director-VET'd (2026-08-06, commit 0a6fa4fb2, experiments/data/goal_bearing_modern_eval_v1.jsonl + notes/research_goal_bearing_modern_eval_2026-08-06.md):** 44 items from 7 MODERN corpora (little_women/anne/tom_sawyer/wizard_of_oz goal-dense novels + RACE 2017 + OneStop 2012-15; alice confirmed too episodic, McGuffey DROPPED). Disk-VET'd: 82% OOV outcome-verbs (36/44, the acquisition target), polarity 27met/17unmet (majority-class=0.61), 24 trap (14 distractor_between + 10 recency_trap), 3 real owner-redirects, gender-tagged roster, per-item citation + outcome_in_lexicon flag. **DATA SUFFICIENCY = YES (answered w/ evidence, per-corpus density survey).** **KEY FAIRNESS FINDING (honest, VET-confirmed, load-bearing): real narrative is NOT fully de-biasable like the synthetic fair_v1 -- recency 0.57/0.50 + nearest_subject 0.30/0.25 are DEFEATED, but first_mention 0.80/0.75 + majority 0.73/0.67 stay HIGH because THE GOAL-OWNER IS USUALLY THE PROTAGONIST (first-introduced + most-mentioned) -- a STRUCTURAL property of narrative, NOT a bug (only 5/44 defeat all 4 baselines).** REFRAME OF "FAIR"+"SUCCESS" (Director): the eval scores TWO axes -- (1) POLARITY-TYPING MET/UNMET on the 36 OOV items = where 1b's telicity-acquisition has NON-POSITIONAL value (positional owner-baselines are IRRELEVANT to polarity; fair baseline = majority-class 0.61 + lexicon-abstain; WELL-POWERED); (2) OWNER-selection on the thin 5-item adversarial slice + keep synthetic fair_v1 (62 items, all baselines floor) for owner-DISCRIMINATION power. Brain-consistent: humans ALSO default goal-owner=protagonist absent a cue, so high first_mention is a real regularity not cheating; our mechanism's distinctive value = TYPING outcomes (baselines can't) + adversarial owners. NEXT: FORMALIZE increment 1b (dispatched) = acquire OOV verb's RESULT-VERB-CLASS/telicity via construction cues (Channel A redirected -- arg-structure DOES predict telicity, unlike valence) -> feed the ALREADY-WIRED congruence_outcome_valence (goal desired-state vs outcome result-class) -> score POLARITY on the 36 OOV-in-goal-context items vs majority-class/lexicon baseline. Also surfaced: some GOAL verbs OOV too (persuade/apologize/avoid/like/love/make).

## 🧱 ACQUISITION INCREMENT 1B = HARD_FAIL #2 -> CONVERGENT DRILL: WE'VE HIT THE GROUNDING WALL (2026-08-06, commit a36ee7c35, Director disk-VET'd)
Built the SHAPE-corrected 1b (single-channel structural: acquire OOV verb's result-class via clause read -> existing congruence organ -> MET/UNMET; pole-sentinel fix; no reward-theta). **HARD_FAIL on 3 axes (measured, honest, no corners -- 3 temptations resisted+reported): primary 16/36=0.444 <= majority 0.639 (unmet_recall COLLAPSED 2/13); noise leak 2/8 (answered/carried->POS); GATE 5 eligible 7/18=0.389 <= fallback 9/18=0.500 -> the SHAPE-fix's central claim FALSIFIED (goal-context path does WORSE than flat fallback); scramble collapses 0.444->0.367 (clean); enrichment delta -0.028 (not load-bearing, predicted); insufficient_corpus 0/36 (well-mined, NOT underpowered).** Cert 220/3 before+after (strict-ADD empty overlay). **ROOT CAUSE (Director disk-CONFIRMED via the acquired-verb polarity map): the verb-lemma-blind clause-structural read measures TRANSITIVITY/AGENCY, not RESULT-POLARITY -- almost every verb consolidates POS (refuse->POS, spoil->POS, flee->POS, croak->POS; only curve/jell/rap/ruin got NEG); any transitive-animate frame fires agonist_realized. MIN_CONFIRM=2 can't gate a consistently-transitive verb (increment 1's dropped AND-gate had MASKED this).** 
**🔑 CONVERGENT DRILL ACROSS BOTH HARD_FAILS (the load-bearing finding): outcome RESULT-VALENCE is a GROUNDED LEXICAL-SEMANTIC property that CANNOT be shortcut -- not from surface structure (1b: transitivity != result-polarity), not from a reward-constant on goal-less input (1). Both failures = the audit's gap #1 "abstract-symbolic -> GROUNDED" LOAD-BEARING WALL, now EMPIRICALLY CONFIRMED TWICE with sharp diagnostics. You cannot learn "waste/spoil/refuse = bad outcome" from the SYNTAX of a sentence using the verb -- it is grounded WORLD-KNOWLEDGE of what the verb DOES. This is "you can't learn revenge from a book" AT THE VERB-SEMANTICS LEVEL.**
**BRAIN'S WAY (the how): a 6yo BRINGS grounded result-verb knowledge TO reading; they do NOT acquire it from the passage. Reading ASSUMES a grounded foundation of common-verb result-semantics.** MISSING COMPONENT (route: missing-LEARNING/missing-grounded-FACT): a grounded result-state feature per verb -- which must be GROUNDED (experience/simulation) or SUPPLIED (a grounded lexicon = the "6yo knows these" early-acquired seed, invariant-OK per supply-DATA-not-mechanism + the authorized 6yo-grounded-foundation pivot).
**STRATEGIC FORK (surfaced to USER, needs their call -- NOT auto-dispatched): (A) REFRAME as FOUNDATION-SUPPLY -- supply grounded result-semantics for the ~few-hundred common verbs a 6yo knows (DATA, invariant-OK, = the authorized grounded-foundation seed, NOT the hand-tagging treadmill nor the reading MECHANISM); unblocks real-prose NOW; acquisition-loop then scoped to genuinely-RARE novel verbs only. (B) pursue GROUNDING result-valence via experiential simulation (audit's deep wall, hard, arbitrary-verb grounding unsolved). (C) accept OOV result-valence out of reach, focus on covered verbs + working mechanisms. DIRECTOR LEAN: (A) -- it IS the brain's way (6yo HAS this knowledge, supplied by development; we supply as data since we can't give embodied years), invariant-consistent, unblocks progress, correctly scopes the loop.** Registry grounded_word_acquisition_loop_increment1 stays SHELVE (sharper falsification banked). The 2 HARD_FAILs were NOT wasted: they diagnosed the real answer (grounding-foundational, not learnable-from-reading) -- the flat-learning-result discipline applied correctly.

## 🎯 DEFINITIVE GROUNDING-WALL DRILL -> REFRAMED DIRECTION (2026-08-06, USER-requested; notes/SYNTHESIS_grounding_wall_definitive_2026-08-06.md + 2 adversarial drills fd9779c84 brain + ccf2de73b ours; Director-VET'd the dissociation claim on disk)
Two fine-toothed adversarial drills (brain machinery + our code) traced "Tom worked all summer to buy a bicycle, but he wasted his savings" through every component. **DEFINITIVE FINDINGS: (1) TWO WALLS ARE SEPARATE + we already cleared one -- FOLLOWING-THE-PLOT (goal-recog/coref/binding/bridging) HARD_PASSed on REAL prose with an UNGROUNDED hand-lexicon (17dd3567b/d157941c6); only KNOWING-WHAT-WORDS-MEAN (grounded lexical valence) is stuck. (2) The direction of "bad" is NOT in the grammar (syntax's "but" gives only "a reversal is coming," never which way) NOR in text-distribution (distributional paradox: antonyms near-identical; distributional valence is PARASITIC on a small hand-seed). It comes from a PRE-LINGUISTIC AFFECTIVE CORE the brain grounds by FEELING (goal-blockage anger endocrine-confirmed by 4-7 MONTHS), onto which the WORD gets mapped years later. (3) THE DECISIVE REFINEMENT: the brain grounds a SMALL primitive anchor + PROPAGATES outward via similarity/opposition (= sentiment-lexicon-from-seed, SentProp/Hamilton) -- it does NOT feel every word.** **SO THE WALL PRECISELY = we OWN the mechanisms (proven) + the propagation machinery (lexical_similarity + OPPOSED_PAIRS) + a SMALL genuinely-earned affective anchor (pfc_gate_cfrpe/appraisal theta), but LACK exactly 2 wires: word->anchor connection + anchor->new-word propagation. Increments 1+1b HARD_FAILed for the RIGHT reason (tried to squeeze good/bad from grammar/reward-constant; it's not there).** **REVISED FORK (supersedes A/B/C): NOT supply-hundreds (treadmill) NOR ground-every-verb (impossible wall) -- but ANCHOR+PROPAGATE: seed a small grounded valence anchor (mostly earned) + wire words to it + propagate to new words via owned similarity/opposition; congruence+bridging (proven) read it in goal context. Brain-faithful, narrow, reuses owned organs. HONEST: a DIRECTION to build+test, not solved (anchor currently grounds situation-types not words; connect+propagate is the unproven build). Awaiting USER call on this reframed direction.**

## 🧩 ANCHOR+PROPAGATE FORMALIZE -> DEFINITIVE DIRECTION VERDICT + EARN-vs-SUPPLY FORK (2026-08-06, commit 3314879f5, notes/research_anchor_propagate_oov_outcome_verb_valence + prereg; Director VET'd the crux on disk)
The USER-greenlit "do it right" formalize confronted the make-or-break DIRECTION question and gave a definitive, disk-verified verdict. **VERDICT (Director-confirmed: ruin/spoil/whitewash -> in_lexicon=False, vec=None): our 4 named organs (lexical_similarity/verb_lexical_similarity/CLASS_REGISTRY/OPPOSED_PAIRS) CANNOT propagate valence to a genuinely untagged word AT ALL -- they are a COMPARATOR between two already-tagged words, with NO encoder from surface-form->vector, and direction is hand-authored INTO the tags. Not merely circular -- a hard blocker. So "spread over our own similarity" is a non-starter for novel verbs.** **A NON-CIRCULAR buildable design DOES exist, needing ONE new piece: WordNet as the valence-BLIND neighborhood channel (already a promoted hdlab dep via animacy_lexicon, invariant-OK DATA; separates synonym from antonym as different relations -> resolves the ruin/fix conflation that killed distributional 1b) + the SHELVED PolarityLexicon._antonyms detector (lift, wire-don't-island) for opposition + the 52-word hand-seed (OUTCOME_SEED_POS/NEG, confirmed 26+26) for DIRECTION. Propagation = kNN-vote over WordNet from the seed, opposition-first. P=0.42.** HARD non-circularity gates pre-reg'd (scramble-seed->collapse, random-graph->collapse, direction-removed->fail). **HONEST CEILING (VET-noted): ~half the 36 eval OOV verbs are LIGHT/support verbs (be/have/go/give/take/make/carry/buy/... 17/33) whose valence is COMPOSITIONAL not verb-level -> no verb-level mechanism types them; abstain. Full-36 ceiling well below 100%.** **THE EARN-vs-SUPPLY FORK (USER's call): this buildable increment PROVES the propagation ARCHITECTURE + gives real open-vocab valence (types never-tagged verbs by SPREADING, not per-word tagging) = consistent with "supply a SMALL seed, EARN the spread" -- BUT its ingredients are SUPPLIED (WordNet neighborhood + 52 hand-labels); it is NOT earned/felt grounding. The genuinely-EARNED version (learn a verb's valence from the situation-model's OWN downstream MET/UNMET consequence via RL -- "feel it") is the DEEPER, separate increment, named + out of scope here. DIRECTOR LEAN: build the WordNet-propagate version FIRST (proves architecture cheaply + real capability + hard non-circularity gates + reuses/ revives owned organs; if it fails even with a supplied seed the RL version won't work either), THEN earn the anchor via RL (swap supplied seed for RL-earned, reuse the propagation machinery). Surfaced to USER -- awaiting call on supply-tolerance (WordNet-propagate now vs hold for purely-earned RL).**

## 🔄 CONSEQUENCE-LEARNING LOOP FORMALIZE -> READY TO BUILD + HONEST CORRECTION (2026-08-06, commit a5f16a5ca, notes/research_consequence_learning_loop_oov_outcome_verb_valence + prereg; Director VET'd)
USER chose the EARNED CONTINUOUS learning system ("build it as we go along") over static WordNet-supply. Formalize delivered, Director-VET'd. **DESIGN: read goal-bearing stories -> when an OOV outcome verb appears, LEARN its result-valence from the episode's computed MET/UNMET CONSEQUENCE, cross-situationally accumulated with anti-drift; light-verbs (be/go/make) correctly converge to GROUNDED_NEUTRAL (the payoff static approaches can't do). Credit-assignment = the outcome-clause verb whose referent LINKS (existing _referent_links) to the goal's referent (excludes bystanders STRUCTURALLY). Reuses find_desired_state/congruence_decision/lexicon_predict/register_acquired_outcome + self-extension loop's multi-pass read->mint->re-read control-flow + decide_keep_or_revert abstain-band (new 3-way POS/NEG/NEUTRAL wrapper).** **🔧 HONEST CORRECTION (Director VET-confirmed on disk, corrects a claim I propagated all session): the RL-earned appraisal family is the WRONG mechanism for word-valence -- pfc_gate_cfrpe is a Go/NoGo PLANNING/SR gate (reach_value/successor, confirmed), context_grounded_valence is GOVERNOR-verb-lexicon-gated (imports exp_bridge1_governor_grounding, not word-identity-independent). So "we own a reward-earned grounding usable here" was OVERSTATED. The RIGHT teacher = congruence_decision's OWN structural MET/UNMET verdict (the story's computed goal-outcome), bootstrapped by the ~90-word seed. This is CLEANER + more honest (learns from the actual story outcome, not a toy sim) -- the toy-world theta grounds a wordless world + can't wire to real-text word learning.** Still genuinely EARNED (OOV verbs learn from story outcomes cross-situationally); the small seed stands in for the felt affective core we can't give it (= "supply small innate seed, EARN the rest"). **WordNet NOT needed anywhere (the earned loop stands alone).** DATA (live scan, 4 novels): 1641/24831 (6.6%) goal-clear sentences, 848 (51.7%) with a computable teacher from the ~90-word seed -> modest but real signal; coverage limited to verbs with >=N exposures (honest ceiling). ## 🔑 USER DIRECTION (2026-08-06): THE LEARNING TOOL = DICTIONARY-LOOKUP + CONSEQUENCE-LEARNING (resolves earn-vs-supply)
USER: "the learning tool can't figure out what the word means from the story on its own -- you need to build that up. the learning tool should be able to LOOK UP THE WORD IN THE DICTIONARY to get a sense as part of that process." **THIS UNIFIES the two threads I wrongly framed as a FORK: the DICTIONARY LOOKUP = the anchor+propagate WordNet design (notes/research_anchor_propagate_..., 3314879f5) -- DENSE, gives an initial sense/valence for EVERY OOV verb (waste -> WordNet near-syn squander/misuse, antonym save/conserve, seed save=POS -> waste~NEG). The CONSEQUENCE-LEARNING loop (a892153ea, validated-but-starved, teacher fires ~15/1431) = the EARNED refinement, sparse but grounded. THE LEARNING TOOL USES BOTH: dictionary as the dense PRIOR, consequence as the earned CONFIRM/CORRECT.** FIXES the INSUFFICIENT_YIELD directly (dictionary covers every word -> loop never starved; consequence confirms/refines where the story gives signal). EARN-vs-SUPPLY RECONCILED (honest): dictionary = SUPPLIED data (WordNet, already a promoted hdlab dep via animacy_lexicon, invariant-OK lexicon-not-mechanism); consequence = EARNED. Combined = definitional prior + experiential grounding = exactly how a human learns a word (look it up AND see how it plays out; when experience contradicts the definition, experience wins). BRAIN-FAITHFUL (multiple word-learning routes: ostension/definition + syntactic bootstrapping + cross-situational/consequence). **NEXT: build the COMBINED learning tool -- WordNet dictionary-lookup (dense prior, the anchor+propagate mechanism) + consequence-learning (earned refinement), combined (agree->confident; conflict->experience refines; no-signal->dictionary prior). Formalize the combination weighting once the in-flight Signal-A-primary test lands (measures the earned component's yield), then build. The anchor+propagate WordNet formalize is NOT superseded -- it is the dictionary-lookup HALF of this tool.**

## ✅ DECISIVE: SIGNAL-A-PRIMARY REFUTED consequence-ONLY + PROVES the USER's dictionary direction (2026-08-06, commit 093ddc1aa, Director disk-VET'd)
Signal-A-primary re-score (drop the over-strict AND-gate, congruence as sole teacher, SCORE grounded words on eval). **HARD_FAIL (grounds-WRONG), decisive: primary 0.1667->0.1944 (noise), grounded_verb_polarity_match_rate 0.333 (BELOW chance), learnable_subset 0/3. n_grounded=11 (all genuinely OOV, non-circ CLEAN: seed_leaks=0, noise_consol=0) but only 3 overlap the eval + ALL 3 are LIGHT verbs (be/make/take; make+take grounded UNMET but gold MET = WRONG).** **THE DEEPER, DECISIVE DIAGNOSIS (Director VET'd): teacher is NOT sparse in aggregate -- Signal-A fires on 112/1431 windows (bootstrap grew 12->85->112). The failure is DISTRIBUTIONAL: density concentrates on HIGH-FREQ LIGHT verbs; the ones barely reaching MIN_CONFIRM=3 get POLARITY-LOCKED by small-sample skew (be/make 0/3), while ZERO genuine CONTENT outcome verbs (hoist/glower/squander/bedew...) EVER reach MIN_CONFIRM -- each recurs only 1-2x across 4 novels. So consequence-alone grounds the WRONG (frequent-function) words WRONG + can't reach the RIGHT (rare-content) words at all. Loop-internal knobs can't win (raise MIN_CONFIRM -> stops false-lock but further starves content verbs).** **THIS IS HARD EVIDENCE FOR THE USER'S DICTIONARY+CONSEQUENCE DIRECTION: content verbs are too RARE in any corpus to ground from repetition -- a human LOOKS THEM UP the first time. The dictionary (WordNet) gives DENSE coverage of exactly the rare content words consequence CANNOT reach; consequence then confirms/refines. Multi-organ densification alone would NOT fix this (doesn't raise per-content-lemma exposure).** Cert 220/3 (engine byte-identical). NEXT: formalize+build the COMBINED tool (dictionary prior + consequence refinement) -- now evidence-backed.

## 🧭 COMBINED DICTIONARY+CONSEQUENCE TOOL -> HARD_FAIL + THE DEEP REFRAME: MET/UNMET IS NOT VERB VALENCE (2026-08-06, commit 329f01733, Director disk-VET'd incl a correction of my OWN VET)
Built the USER-directed combined tool (WordNet dictionary pseudo-count prior + consequence refinement, Bayesian inject-once). **HARD_FAIL, decisive, no-corners. 3-WAY ABLATION (Director VET'd on disk): dictionary-only 0.2222 / consequence-only 0.1944 / COMBINED 0.1944 (majority floor 0.6389, empty floor 0.1667). Combined is WORSE than dict-alone (-0.028) + identical to consequence-alone; combined-vs-consequence-scrambled = NEGATIVE marginal -> the assembly is effectively dict-noise.** Content-16: only 3/16 got a dictionary hit (encore/jell correct [jell by WSD-luck], improve WRONG); 13/16 ABSTAIN (sparse WordNet antonyms + sub-margin neighbors). **🔑 THE DEEP FINDING (reframes the whole recent arc): LEXICAL-VALENCE != GOAL-CONGRUENCE. Where the dictionary DID fire correctly (improve->POS lexically), it is the WRONG answer because the eval scores GOAL-congruence ('was the DESIRED improve ACHIEVED?') and that item is UNMET. Even a PERFECT dictionary of verb valence cannot solve MET/UNMET, because MET/UNMET is a GOAL-RELATIONAL composite: (a) the verb's result-pole + (b) whether the outcome ACTUALLY HAPPENED (negation/failure/attempt-vs-achievement) + (c) the goal-relation. The entire recent 'ground the verb's valence' program (structural-read / reward-constant / consequence-learning / dictionary / combined = 5 decisive HARD_FAILs) attacks only (a).** **🔧 MY VET CORRECTED (own it): I told USER 'light-verbs naturally won't false-lock' from a SHALLOW [:4]-synset check showing make antonyms=[]. WRONG -- full WordNet has make<->break, find<->lose, take<->refuse; break/lose are NEG anchors -> Stage-A opposition@conf1.0 -> find self-locks POS with ZERO real exposures (gate-6 violation), make polar-locks POS. The dict priors DEGRADED light-verb safety (6 polar-locked). VET-the-VET vindicated.** Cert 220/3 (inject default-None additive). No corners (inject-once verified, thresholds not tuned, make/find self-lock reported not suppressed). **STRATEGIC JUNCTURE (recommend the step-back USER offered): the evidence now DEFINITIVELY shows typing OOV-outcome MET/UNMET by grounding the VERB'S valence hits a fundamental decomposition wall -- MET/UNMET isn't verb valence. REFRAME HYPOTHESIS: the '(b) did-it-happen' component (negation/failure/prevented/achieved, often EXPLICIT in text -> structurally detectable, e.g. improve-UNMET = the improve didn't happen) may be a HIGHER-yield + more tractable lever than verb-valence-grounding; we just built negation-scope for GOALS + could extend to OUTCOMES. NEXT: analyze the eval's MET/UNMET composition (how many items are driven by verb-pole vs did-it-happen vs goal-relation) to ground the reframe, THEN decide direction with USER. NOT auto-dispatched -- this is a talk-strategy moment.**

## 📊 EVAL DRIVER-DECOMPOSITION -> EVIDENCE-GROUNDED DIRECTION (2026-08-06, notes/research_goal_bearing_eval_driver_decomposition_2026-08-06.md, Director-VET'd per-item table + spot-checks + counts)
Per-item read of what DRIVES each gold MET/UNMET on the 36 OOV items. **DISTRIBUTION (Director-confirmed by reading the table + counting): DID-IT-HAPPEN (B) 15/36 (42%) = largest primary; GOAL-RELATION (C) 13/36 (36%); OTHER/composite (D) 6/36 (17%); VERB-POLE (A) 2/36 (6%).** **DEFINITIVE: the verb-valence-grounding line we spent 5 HARD_FAILs on has a HARD 6% CEILING -- and is actively WRONG on numeric-threshold items (race_chen_situps 'improve'->reads POS but 30<35 goal-bar=UNMET; onestop_carle 'be'->96.9% but goal wanted 100%). Independent confirmation: 4/8 IN-LEXICON controls are ALSO dict-wrong (negation+owner), so verb-valence caps well under 100% even on the EASY subset.** **REFRAME HOLDS + SHARPENED: did-it-happen is ~8x verb-pole's ceiling (47% generous / ~22% standalone-clean) BUT needs goal-relation/owner-attribution as a REQUIRED COMPANION on 7/15 of its own items -> DO NOT build a standalone did-it-happen scanner; WIRE DID-IT-HAPPEN AS A SIGNAL INTO THE EXISTING GOAL-CONGRUENCE/OWNER-BINDING ORGAN (congruence_decision). Goal-relation is the biggest TOTAL footprint (primary-or-companion on 25/36=69%).** IMPORTANT SUBTLETIES the analysis surfaced (did-it-happen is NOT naive negation-scanning): exception-scope ('all failed EXCEPT the one that curved'), false-negative-OVERRIDE ('so she had failed'... later 'winner of the Avery'), multi-attempt persistence ('tried... but slow'... 'made 400,000'), final-state reading ('German was back'xN... 'never came back'). BRAIN-FOUNDATIONAL fit: this moves from grading WORDS to reading the SITUATION-STATE relative to the goal (what the brain does); did-it-happen (occurrence of the wanted thing) is the missing layer the brain always tracks + is often EXPLICIT in text (the tractable slice). NEXT: build did-it-happen (occurrence/negation/failure/achievement, with the 4 subtleties) as an INPUT to congruence_decision; reuse coref for the owner-attribution companion. Verb-valence grounding SHELVED (6% ceiling, evidence-closed). (Stray legacy-convention handoff file exp_dev_handoff_*eval_driver* removed -- content is in the research note; ferry mechanism is dead per CLAUDE.md.)

## 🚨 GENERALIZATION PROBE = DIRECTION-RESHAPING NEGATIVE (2026-08-06, commit f496caa51, exp_real_text_goal_owner_generalization_diagnostic_v1, Director disk-VET incl gold soundness)
Ran the wired composed organ (select_outcome_owner + outcome-valence congruence) on 10 REAL McGuffey grade-1/2/3 narrative passages (gold hand-authored BEFORE running, honestly selected, all 10 have real owners+goals -- gold VET'd sound). **RESULT: organ owner-acc 0.30 (3/10) LOSES to EVERY positional baseline (recency/first_mention 0.70, majority 0.60); polarity 0.10 == lexicon floor. THE WIRED ORGANS DO NOT GENERALIZE to real prose.** Failure tally: owner OUTCOME_NEVER_TYPED=6/10 + TIEBREAK_MISS=1; polarity LEXICON_COVERAGE_MISS=9/10. **DOMINANT BOTTLENECK (VET-confirmed, precise): CLOSED-SET hand-typed verb lexicons (V2_OUTCOME_MET/_UNMET ~23 words, CLASS_REGISTRY ~12 classes, desiderative set) are OOV for real prose -> outcome/goal TYPING NEVER FIRES -> owner-selection blocked before its reasoning engages (e.g. mg2_henry: GOAL 'wanted to help' types, but OUTCOME 'praise/accept' is OOV in V2_OUTCOME -> OUTCOME_NEVER_TYPED).** This was the REAL blocker all along -- NOT event-hood, NOT role-assignment (those were real but non-dominant); the hand-authored-instrument HARD_PASSes did NOT transfer because the instruments used in-lexicon verbs. NUANCE: real text isn't trap-structured so recency is naturally strong (0.70); but the decisive fact is the organ is BLOCKED (6/10 never fire), not merely out-competed. BRAIN-DRILL: closed `lemma in members` vs the ATL hub's OPEN-vocab graded shared-feature similarity (novel verb classified by feature-similarity to exemplars). **FIX = REUSE (wire-don't-island): extend verb-class membership from `lemma in members` to concept_similarity(verb, class_exemplars) >= threshold, using the ALREADY-PROVEN+WIRED ATL organ (hdlab/lexical_similarity.py) -- currently wired only for REFERENT-linking, not verb-class membership. This VINDICATES the ATL hub as the GENERAL open-vocab solution across the pipeline.** Requires feature-coverage for real-prose verbs (the ATL feature-coverage/induction frontier -- now the CRITICAL PATH to generalization, not a side follow-up). **DIRECTION RESHAPED: from component-fixes-on-instruments -> OPEN-VOCAB verb-typing via ATL similarity = the generalization critical path.**
